## `AGO-5310-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AGO` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Cross-Belt-Matrix Specialist
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for National Postal Hub Cross-Belt Sorting Machine
- **Intro:** Cross-Belt-Matrix Specialist — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for National Postal Hub Cross-Belt Sorting Machine — Pre-Start Authorization and PPE Verification: PPE must be worn at all times; no exceptions. LOTO station must have at least 3 padlocks and 3 hasps available.
